;*******************************************************************************************************************************
;** Coverage: display rounding under DRM in the UNIT format. UNIT rounds in the ENG block and ROUND keeps what the line       **
;** shows; RXT checks the stored text. DRM 0 to 6; UNIT 0, 2 and 5; values shown without a prefix, with an SI prefix and      **
;** with a power of ten, the six points of drm_round_cov, positive and negative.                                              **
;** Expected: Python decimal, probe/drm_model.py and probe/gen_drm_ext_cov.py.                                                **
;*******************************************************************************************************************************
Acc: 34
In: FL_SPCRES=0 FL_CPXRES=0 SD=0 RMODE=0 IM=2compl SS=4 WS=64 FL_FRACT=0

; ROUND renders the X line, and a TI left by the reset or an earlier file replaces it: CLRMOD clears it first
Item: ITM_CLRMOD
In:  FL_ASLIFT=0 RX=Real:"1"
Out: EC=0

Func: fnRound

; F5, fixed: a value shown with an SI prefix. exponentToUnitDisplayString (addons.c) writes the power of ten of the prefix into displayValueX, so ROUND
; stores the value the line shows: 12.35E3 for 12345.678 under UNIT 3.
; F6, fixed: a value beyond the prefixes. The exponent reaches displayValueX after an e, so 3.14 x 10^18 is stored as 3.14E18.
